PAGE TEXT:
إعدادات الأمان بيان حقوق النشر تسجيل الدخول إلى صفحة الإدارة وضع الرموز الإعداد السريع SkyTone الاتصال VPN إحصاءات حركة المرور الرسائل النصية SMS USSD تحديث مشاركة بطاقة microSD إدارة رقم التعريف الشخصي(PIN) إعدادات شبكة WLAN إعدادات DHCP إعدادات الأمان إدارة النظام الأسئلة المتداولة بادئات واختصارات إعدادات الأمان تمكين الجدار الناري أو تعطيله يدعم dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); وظيفة الجدار الناري للتحكم في نقل تدفقات البيانات ولحماية شبكة المنطقة المحلية الخاصة بك من الوصول غير المصرح به. تعيين تصفية عنوان IP لشبكة LAN يمكن استخدام وظيفة تصفية عناوين IP في شبكة المنطقة المحلية (LAN) لمنع عملاء معينين في شبكة LAN من الوصول إلى بعض الخدمات على شبكة الإنترنت. تكوين خادم افتراضي يدعم dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); الخادم الافتراضي لتمكين المستخدمين الخارجيين من استخدام الخدمات المتوفرة في شبكة المنطقة المحلية (LAN) التي تستخدم بروتوكول نقل النص التشعبي (HTTP) بروتوكول نقل الملفات (FTP) والبروتوكولات الأخرى. تكوين تطبيق خاص يدعم dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); وظيفة استخدام تطبيق خاص لتكوين إعادة توجيه المنافذ الديناميكية. يتعين على تطبيقات معينة في شبكة المنطقة المحلية (LAN) استخدام منفذ محدد في الجدار الناري للوصول إلى تطبيقات بعيدة. لإعداد اتصال بروتوكول التحكم بالنقل وبروتوكول وحدة بيانات المستخدم (TCP/UDP) بين أحد التطبيقات في شبكة LAN وتطبيق بعيد، فإن الجدار الناري يستخدم وظيفة إعادة توجيه المنافذ هذه لفتح المنفذ المطلوب. تعيين DMZ إذا كان يتعذر على المستخدمين الخارجيين الوصول إلى خدمات معينة في الشبكة تقدمها شبكة المنطقة المحلية (LAN)، استخدم وظيفة DMZ التي يقدمها dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); لتعيين العميل الذي يقدم خدمات الشبكة المطلوبة كمضيف DMZ؛ بعد ذلك يكون بمقدور المستخدمين الخارجيين الوصول إلى هذه الخدمات بصورة ملائمة. يعتبر DMZ اختصارًا لكلمة المنطقة المحايدة في الشبكات. تعيين ALG لـ SIP بروتوكول بدء الجلسة (SIP) هو بروتوكول تحكم في طبقة التطبيقات. ويُستخدم هذا البروتوكول لبدء جلسة أو لتغييرها أو لإنهائها. بوابة مستوى التطبيق (ALG) عبارة عن تطبيق خاص لبروتوكول بدء الجلسة ويُستخدم للتحقق من حالة حزم البيانات. لإتمام تطبيق SIP، قم بتمكين ALG الخاص بـ SIP. تعيين UPnP تعمل خدمة التوصيل والتشغيل العالمي (UPnP) على تحقيق اتصال ذكي بين اثنين من أجهزة UPnP باستخدام وظيفة إعادة توجيه المنافذ. وتستطيع أجهزة UPnP الحصول على عناوين IP تلقائيًا والوصول إلى الإنترنت بسرعة فائقة. تكوين ترجمة عنوان شبكة الاتصال (NAT) ترجمة عنوان شبكة الاتصال هي عملية تعديل مصدر عناوين IP ووجهتها وذلك عندما يتم إرسال حزم IP عبر جهاز توجيه (راوتر) أو جدار حماية. والغرض من هذه العملية هو ترجمة عناوين IP الداخلية (خاصة) إلى عناوين IP خارجية (عامة) لتوفير حلاً لمشاكل استهلاك عناوين IP. إن dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); يدعم ترجمة عنوان شبكة الاتصال (NAT) بالمخروط المقيد بمنفذ ومتماثل NAT. يمكنك تكوين إعدادات NAT كما هو مطلوب. تصفية الأجهزة المحددة تستطيع وظيفة فلترة عنوان MAC الخاصة بجهاز dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); تصفية الأجهزة المحددة الموجودة على الشبكة المحلية لجهاز dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); وتصبح هذه الأجهزة ممنوعة من الاتصال بالإنترنت أو الوصول إلى الأجهزة الأخرى الموجودة على نفس الشبكة. الموضوع الرئيسي: دليل المستخدم

إعداد وضع المشاركة عبر الويب بيان حقوق النشر تسجيل الدخول إلى صفحة الإدارة وضع الرموز الإعداد السريع SkyTone الاتصال VPN إحصاءات حركة المرور الرسائل النصية SMS USSD تحديث مشاركة بطاقة microSD إدارة رقم التعريف الشخصي(PIN) إعدادات شبكة WLAN إعدادات DHCP إعدادات الأمان إدارة النظام الأسئلة المتداولة بادئات واختصارات إعداد وضع المشاركة عبر الويب يمكنك إعداد dw(sd_label_access_type); و dw(sd_shared_path); ضمن dw(sd_label_web_share_mode); . الإجراء انقر على dw(sharing_label_sharing); . ضمن dw(sd_label_sd_card_sharing); ، حدد dw(sd_label_web_share_mode); . ضمن dw(sd_websharemode_settings); ، قم بتعيين وضع مشاركة الويب. قم بتعيين dw(sd_label_access_type); . dw(sd_label_read_only); : يمكن الاطلاع على الملفات المشتركة فقط. dw(sd_label_read_write); : يمكن الاطلاع على الملفات المشتركة وإدارتها. قم بتعيين dw(sd_shared_path); . dw(sd_label_entire_sd_card); : يشير إلى أنه يمكن الوصول إلى جميع الملفات المخزنة في بطاقة microSD. dw(sd_label_custom_setting); : يشير إلى أنه يمكن الوصول إلى بعض الملفات المحددة في بطاقة microSD. انقر على dw(firewall_label_select); ، وحدد المجلدات في خانة الحوار dw(sd_hint_select_a_folder); لمشاركة الملفات في هذه المجلدات. انقر على dw(common_apply); . الموضوع الرئيسي: الوصول إلى بطاقة microSD عبر الشبكة

تحميل الملفات لمشاركتها بيان حقوق النشر تسجيل الدخول إلى صفحة الإدارة وضع الرموز الإعداد السريع SkyTone الاتصال VPN إحصاءات حركة المرور الرسائل النصية SMS USSD تحديث مشاركة بطاقة microSD إدارة رقم التعريف الشخصي(PIN) إعدادات شبكة WLAN إعدادات DHCP إعدادات الأمان إدارة النظام الأسئلة المتداولة بادئات واختصارات تحميل الملفات لمشاركتها عندما يتم تعيين نوع وصول dw(sd_label_web_share_mode); إلى dw(sd_label_read_write); ، يمكنك إنشاء مجلد في بطاقة microSD، وتحميل الملفات المحلية إليه. الإجراء انقر على dw(sharing_label_sharing); . ضمن dw(sd_label_sd_card_sharing); ، حدد dw(sd_label_web_share_mode); . ضمن dw(sd_websharemode_settings); : لتحديد مسار موجود، انقر فوق اسم المجلد في dw(firewall_label_name); العمود، وقم بالبحث عن مسار الملفات المراد تحميلها. لإنشاء المجلد: انقر على dw(firewall_label_name); ، وحدد مسار المجلد الجديد. انقر على dw(sd_label_new_folder); . في خانة الحوار dw(sd_label_new_folder); ، أدخل اسما للمجلد الجديد. انقر على dw(common_ok); . انقر على dw(sd_label_upload); . في خانة الحوار dw(sd_label_upload_file); ، انقر على dw(common_browse); ، وحدد الملفات المحلية لتحميلها. انقر على dw(sd_label_upload); . الموضوع الرئيسي: الوصول إلى بطاقة microSD عبر الشبكة

عرض وتحميل الملفات المشتركة بيان حقوق النشر تسجيل الدخول إلى صفحة الإدارة وضع الرموز الإعداد السريع SkyTone الاتصال VPN إحصاءات حركة المرور الرسائل النصية SMS USSD تحديث مشاركة بطاقة microSD إدارة رقم التعريف الشخصي(PIN) إعدادات شبكة WLAN إعدادات DHCP إعدادات الأمان إدارة النظام الأسئلة المتداولة بادئات واختصارات عرض وتحميل الملفات المشتركة يمكنك عرض وتحميل الملفات المشتركة والمخزنة على بطاقة microSD. الإجراء انقر على dw(sharing_label_sharing); . ضمن dw(sd_label_sd_card_sharing); ، حدد dw(sd_label_web_share_mode); . ضمن dw(sd_shared_list); ، انقر فوق المجلد لعرض الملفات المشتركة فيه. ملاحظة: انقر على dw(sd_label_up); للعودة إلى المجلد الأصل الذي يتم عرضه حالياً. انقر على dw(sd_label_view_microsd_files); في صفحة تسجيل الدخول للوصول إلى بطاقة microSD. انقر على dw(down_label_download); لتحميل ملف مشترك. الموضوع الرئيسي: الوصول إلى بطاقة microSD عبر الشبكة

توصيل الجهاز بالكمبيوتر باستخدام كابل USB بيان حقوق النشر تسجيل الدخول إلى صفحة الإدارة وضع الرموز الإعداد السريع SkyTone الاتصال VPN إحصاءات حركة المرور الرسائل النصية SMS USSD تحديث مشاركة بطاقة microSD إدارة رقم التعريف الشخصي(PIN) إعدادات شبكة WLAN إعدادات DHCP إعدادات الأمان إدارة النظام الأسئلة المتداولة بادئات واختصارات توصيل الجهاز بالكمبيوتر باستخدام كابل USB للوصول إلى الملفات المخزنة على بطاقة microSD، تحتاج إلى توصيل dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); بالكمبيوتر باستخدام كابل USB. السياق في الإجراء التالي على سبيل المثال يُستخدم نظام التشغيل مايكروسوفت ويندوز 7. الإجراء قم بتوصيل الجهاز بجهاز الكمبيوتر عن طريق كابل USB متوافق. بعد أن يقوم الكمبيوتر بالكشف عن الجهاز الجديد، اختر ابدأ > كمبيوتر . انقر نقراً مزدوجاً فوق جهاز التخزين القابل للإزالة المعروض والمطابق لبطاقة microSD. قم بعرض وإدارة الملفات في بطاقة microSD. الموضوع الرئيسي: الوصول إلى بطاقة microSD باستخدام كبل USB

الوصول إلى بطاقة microSD عبر الشبكة بيان حقوق النشر تسجيل الدخول إلى صفحة الإدارة وضع الرموز الإعداد السريع SkyTone الاتصال VPN إحصاءات حركة المرور الرسائل النصية SMS USSD تحديث مشاركة بطاقة microSD إدارة رقم التعريف الشخصي(PIN) إعدادات شبكة WLAN إعدادات DHCP إعدادات الأمان إدارة النظام الأسئلة المتداولة بادئات واختصارات الوصول إلى بطاقة microSD عبر الشبكة عندما يتم تمكين dw(sd_label_web_share_mode); ، يمكنك الوصول إلى بطاقة microSD من صفحة إدارة الويب. إعداد وضع المشاركة عبر الويب يمكنك إعداد dw(sd_label_access_type); و dw(sd_shared_path); ضمن dw(sd_label_web_share_mode); . تحميل الملفات لمشاركتها عندما يتم تعيين نوع وصول dw(sd_label_web_share_mode); إلى dw(sd_label_read_write); ، يمكنك إنشاء مجلد في بطاقة microSD، وتحميل الملفات المحلية إليه. عرض وتحميل الملفات المشتركة يمكنك عرض وتحميل الملفات المشتركة والمخزنة على بطاقة microSD. حذف الملفات المشتركة عندما يتم تعيين نوع وصول dw(sd_label_web_share_mode); إلى dw(sd_label_read_write); ، يمكنك حذف المجلدات المخزنة في بطاقة microSD. الموضوع الرئيسي: مشاركة بطاقة microSD

إعداد وظيفة إحصائيات حركة المرور الشهرية بيان حقوق النشر تسجيل الدخول إلى صفحة الإدارة وضع الرموز الإعداد السريع SkyTone الاتصال VPN إحصاءات حركة المرور الرسائل النصية SMS USSD تحديث مشاركة بطاقة microSD إدارة رقم التعريف الشخصي(PIN) إعدادات شبكة WLAN إعدادات DHCP إعدادات الأمان إدارة النظام الأسئلة المتداولة بادئات واختصارات إعداد وظيفة إحصائيات حركة المرور الشهرية يمكنك إعداد وظيفة إحصائيات حركة المرور الشهرية وعرض حركة مرور الشبكة للشهر الحالي. الإجراء انقر فوق dw(connection_hilink_label_traffic_statistics); . انقر فوق dw(IDS_statistics_aglie_set_package); . يتم عرض مربع الحوار dw(IDS_statistics_aglie_set_package_dialog); . قم بإعداد معلمات إحصائيات حركة المرور الشهرية. ملاحظة: إذا تجاوز استخدام حركة المرور الخاصة بك في الشهر الحالي dw(IDS_statistics_aglie_data_threshold); من dw(IDS_statistics_aglie_set_data_volume_mobile); ، يتغير لون الشريط في صفحة dw(connection_hilink_label_traffic_statistics); . انتبه إلى استخدام حركة المرور الخاصة بك لتجنب تكبد تكاليف بيانات عالية. انقر فوق dw(common_save); . ملاحظة: انقر فوق dw(IDS_statistics_aglie_edit_month_volue); لتعديل بارامترات إحصائيات حركة المرور الشهرية. الموضوع الرئيسي: إحصاءات حركة المرور

تنفيذ اختبار اتصال الشبكة (ping) بيان حقوق النشر تسجيل الدخول إلى صفحة الإدارة وضع الرموز الإعداد السريع SkyTone الاتصال VPN إحصاءات حركة المرور الرسائل النصية SMS USSD تحديث مشاركة بطاقة microSD إدارة رقم التعريف الشخصي(PIN) إعدادات شبكة WLAN إعدادات DHCP إعدادات الأمان إدارة النظام الأسئلة المتداولة بادئات واختصارات تنفيذ اختبار اتصال الشبكة (ping) في حال تعذر اتصال جهاز dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); بالإنترنت، قم بتنفيذ اختبار اتصال الشبكة (ping) لمعرفة الأسباب المحتملة. الإجراء اختر dw(common_settings); > dw(system_label_system); > dw(system_label_diagnosis); . حدد dw(IDS_system_label_diagnosis_method_ping); من القائمة المنسدلة لـ dw(IDS_system_diagnosis_method_name); . أدخل عنوان IP أو اسم النطاق في خانة النص لـ dw(IDS_system_label_diagnosis_targetip_domain); ، على سبيل المثال www.google.com. قم بتعيين dw(IDS_system_label_diagnosis_packetsize); و dw(IDS_system_label_diagnosis_timeout); . قم بتحديد أو إلغاء تحديد dw(IDS_system_label_diagnosis_fragment_remind); . ملاحظة: حدد dw(IDS_system_label_diagnosis_fragment_remind); في حال تعيين dw(IDS_system_label_diagnosis_packetsize); إلى قيمة أكبر من القيمة الإفتراضية. انقر فوق dw(common_apply); . سيتم عرض نتائج التشخيص في منطقة dw(IDS_diagnosis_result_title); في أسفل الصفحة. الموضوع الرئيسي: أعطال اتصال الشبكة التي يمكن تشخيصها

إدارة النظام بيان حقوق النشر تسجيل الدخول إلى صفحة الإدارة وضع الرموز الإعداد السريع SkyTone الاتصال VPN إحصاءات حركة المرور الرسائل النصية SMS USSD تحديث مشاركة بطاقة microSD إدارة رقم التعريف الشخصي(PIN) إعدادات شبكة WLAN إعدادات DHCP إعدادات الأمان إدارة النظام الأسئلة المتداولة بادئات واختصارات إدارة النظام عرض معلومات من dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); يوضح هذا القسم كيفية عرض المعلومات عن dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); . التبديل بين اللغات يصف هذا القسم كيفية التبديل بين لغات واجهة المستخدم لجهاز محطة dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); . تغيير كلمة المرور لمنع الوصول غير المصرح به إلى صفحة الإدارة، قم بتغيير كلمة مرور تسجيل الدخول. استعادة الإعدادات الافتراضية بعد أن تستعيد dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); لإعداداته الافتراضية، سيتم حذف معلوماتك الشخصية وستتم استعادة المعلمات إلى قيمها الافتراضية. إعادة تمهيد dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); يوضح هذا القسم كيفية إعادة تمهيد dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); . تمكين التمهيد السريع تعمل وظيفة التمهيد السريع على تقليل وقت التمهيد بدرجة كبيرة. أعطال اتصال الشبكة التي يمكن تشخيصها في حال تعذر اتصال جهاز dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); بالإنترنت، استخدم أدوات التشخيص لمعرفة الأسباب المحتملة. الموضوع الرئيسي: دليل المستخدم

أعطال اتصال الشبكة التي يمكن تشخيصها بيان حقوق النشر تسجيل الدخول إلى صفحة الإدارة وضع الرموز الإعداد السريع SkyTone الاتصال VPN إحصاءات حركة المرور الرسائل النصية SMS USSD تحديث مشاركة بطاقة microSD إدارة رقم التعريف الشخصي(PIN) إعدادات شبكة WLAN إعدادات DHCP إعدادات الأمان إدارة النظام الأسئلة المتداولة بادئات واختصارات أعطال اتصال الشبكة التي يمكن تشخيصها في حال تعذر اتصال جهاز dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); بالإنترنت، استخدم أدوات التشخيص لمعرفة الأسباب المحتملة. تنفيذ اختبار اتصال الشبكة (ping) في حال تعذر اتصال جهاز dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); بالإنترنت، قم بتنفيذ اختبار اتصال الشبكة (ping) لمعرفة الأسباب المحتملة. تنفيذ اختبار تتبع المسار في حال تعذر اتصال جهاز dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); بالإنترنت، قم بتنفيذ اختبار تتبع المسار لمعرفة الأسباب المحتملة. الموضوع الرئيسي: إدارة النظام

إعداد حقوق المستخدم بيان حقوق النشر تسجيل الدخول إلى صفحة الإدارة وضع الرموز الإعداد السريع SkyTone الاتصال VPN إحصاءات حركة المرور الرسائل النصية SMS USSD تحديث مشاركة بطاقة microSD إدارة رقم التعريف الشخصي(PIN) إعدادات شبكة WLAN إعدادات DHCP إعدادات الأمان إدارة النظام الأسئلة المتداولة بادئات واختصارات إعداد حقوق المستخدم سيتمكن المستخدمون من الوصول إلى جهاز تخزين USB في حالة تعيين حقوق القراءة/الكتابة والوصول إلى المجلد. الإجراء اختر dw(sharing_label_sharing); > dw(IDS_settingz_samba_user_settings); . انقر فوق dw(common_add); . أدخل اسم المستخدم وكلمة المرور. أدخل كلمة المرور مرة أخرى. حدد dw(IDS_system_samba_user_setting_right); . dw(sd_label_read_only); : يمكن عرض الملفات المشتركة فقط. dw(sd_label_read_write); : يمكن عرض الملفات المشتركة وتعديلها. حدد dw(IDS_system_samba_user_setting_directory); . dw(IDS_system_samba_share_allpath); : يمكن الوصول إلى جميع الملفات المخزنة على جهاز تخزين USB. dw(IDS_system_samba_share_user_path); : يمكن الوصول إلى بعض الملفات المحددة على جهاز التخزين USB. انقر فوق dw(firewall_label_select); وحدد المجلدات في مربع حوار dw(sd_hint_select_a_folder); المعروض. انقر فوق dw(common_ok); . انقر فوق dw(common_apply); . الموضوع الرئيسي: إدارة المستخدمين

PAGE TEXT:
بيان حقوق النشر بيان حقوق النشر تسجيل الدخول إلى صفحة الإدارة وضع الرموز الإعداد السريع SkyTone الاتصال VPN إحصاءات حركة المرور الرسائل النصية SMS USSD تحديث مشاركة بطاقة microSD إدارة رقم التعريف الشخصي(PIN) إعدادات شبكة WLAN إعدادات DHCP إعدادات الأمان إدارة النظام الأسئلة المتداولة بادئات واختصارات بيان حقوق النشر dw(common_copyright.replace(/%s/g, get_copyright())); لا يجوز إعادة إنتاج أي جزء من هذا المستند أو نقله بأي شكل أو بأي وسيلة دون موافقة كتابية مسبقة من شركة Huawei Technologies Co., Ltd. قد يشتمل المنتج الموضح في هذا الدليل على برامج محمية بموجب حقوق النشر لشركة Huawei Technologies Co., Ltd والجهات المرخِّصة. ولا يجوز للعملاء بأية طريقة إعادة إنتاج البرامج سالفة الذكر أو توزيعها أو تعديلها أو إلغاء تجميعها أو فكها أو فك تشفيرها أو استخراجها أو إجراء هندسة عكسية عليها أو تأجيرها أو التنازل عنها أو ترخيصها من الباطن، ما لم تكن هذه القيود محظورة بموجب القوانين المعمول بها أو يوافق أصحاب حقوق النشر المعنيون على هذه الأعمال بموجب تراخيص. العلامات التجارية والتصاريح و و HUAWEI و هي علامات تجارية أو علامات تجارية مُسجلة مملوكة لشركة Huawei Technologies Co., Ltd. أما العلامات التجارية الأخرى وأسماء المنتجات والخدمات والشركات الوارد ذكرها فهي مملوكة لمالكيها المعنيين. ملاحظة تعتمد بعض ميزات المنتج وملحقاته الموضحة في هذا المستند على البرنامج المثبَّت وقدرات وإعدادات الشبكة المحلية، وربما لا يتم تنشيطها أو يتم تحديدها بواسطة مشغلي الشبكة المحلية أو موفري خدمة الشبكة، وبالتالي قد لا تتطابق الأوصاف الوارد ذكرها في هذا المستند بشكل دقيق مع المنتج الذي تشتريه أو ملحقاته. تحتفظ شركة Huawei Technologies Co., Ltd بالحق في تغيير أو تعديل أي معلومات أو مواصفات واردة في هذا الدليل دون إشعار مسبق أو التزام من جانبها. نفي الضمان يتم تقديم محتويات هذا الدليل "بالحالة التي عليها". وباستثناء ما تقضي به القوانين المعمول بها، لا توجد أية ضمانات من أي نوع فيما يتعلق بدقة هذا الدليل أو وثوقيته أو محتوياته، سواء كانت صريحة أو ضمنية، بما في ذلك على سبيل المثال لا الحصر الضمانات الضمنية بالقابلية للتسويق والملاءمة لغرض معين. وإلى أقصى حد يجيزه القانون المعمول به، لا تتحمل شركة HUAWEI TECHNOLOGIES CO., LTD بأي حال المسؤولية عن أي أضرار خاصة أو عارضة أو غير مباشرة أو لاحقة أو خسارة الأرباح أو الأعمال أو الإيرادات أو البيانات أو السمعة أو المدخرات المتوقعة. لوائح الاستيراد والتصدير يتعين على العملاء الالتزام بكافة قوانين ولوائح التصدير أو الاستيراد المعمول بها وكذلك الحصول على كافة التصاريح والتراخيص الحكومية اللازمة لتصدير أو إعادة تصدير أو استيراد المنتج الوارد ذكره في هذا الدليل بما في ذلك البرامج والبيانات الفنية الموجودة فيه. إشعار حقوق الطبع والنشر لاطلاع على مزيدٍ من التفاصيل حول إشعار حقوق النشر والطبع لهذا المنتج، يُرجى زيارة موقع الويب : http://consumer.huawei.com/minisite/copyright_notice/ أو من خلال التواصل عبر البريد الإلكتروني: mobile@huawei.com . الموضوع الرئيسي: دليل المستخدم

Enabling the DHCP Server Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Enabling the DHCP Server If the Dynamic Host Configuration Protocol (DHCP) server is enabled, the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); allocates IP addresses to clients connected to it. Context dw(dhcp_label_dhcp_ip_range); and dw(dhcp_label_dhcp_lease_time); are available to be set only when the DHCP server is enabled. After the DHCP server is enabled, enable the function to automatically obtain an IP address and a DNS server address from clients. Procedure Choose dw(common_settings); > dw(wlan_label_wlan); > dw(dhcp_label_dhcp); . In dw(dialup_label_ip_address); , enter the two last digits of the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); 's IP address. Select dw(common_enable); to enable the DHCP server. In dw(dhcp_label_dhcp_ip_range); , enter the last digit of the start IP address and the end IP address. In dw(dhcp_label_dhcp_lease_time); , enter a lease time. Click dw(common_apply); . Parent topic: DHCP Settings

Configuring DHCP Clients Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Configuring DHCP Clients If the Dynamic Host Configuration Protocol (DHCP) server is enabled, certain settings must be configured on clients. Context The following describe how to configure DHCP settings on a computer running Windows 7 as an example. Procedure Choose Start > Control Panel . From the Viewed by drop-down list, choose Category . Choose Network and Internet > Network and Sharing Center > Change adapter settings > Wireless Network Connection . Right click the network icon that you want to configure and choose Properties . On the Networking tab page, choose Internet Protocol Version 6 (TCP/IPv6) or Internet Protocol Version 4 (TCP/IPv4) , and then click Properties . On the Internet Protocol 6 (TCP/IPv6) Properties dialog box, select Obtain an IPv6 address automatically and Obtain DNS server address automatically . Or on the Internet Protocol 4 (TCP/IPv4) Properties dialog box, select Obtain an IP address automatically and Obtain DNS server address automatically . Click OK . Parent topic: DHCP Settings

PAGE TEXT:
Turning On or Off PIN Verification Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Turning On or Off PIN Verification If PIN verification is turned on, you need to enter the PIN only when the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); is turned on. If PIN verification is turned off, no PIN is required. Context If a PIN is required, enter the correct PIN. If you enter your PIN incorrectly three times consecutively, the SIM/USIM/UIM card is locked and you must enter the PIN unlock key (PUK). If you enter the PUK incorrectly ten times consecutively, the SIM/USIM/UIM card is locked permanently. If you cannot enter the correct PIN or PUK, network-associated functions are unavailable. The PIN and the PUK are provided with the SIM/USIM/UIM card. If you did not receive them or have forgotten them, contact the operator. Procedure Choose dw(common_settings); > dw(firewall_label_security); > dw(dialup_label_pin_code_management); . From dw(dialup_label_pin_code_operation); , select: dw(common_enable); : to turn on PIN verification. dw(common_disable); : to turn off PIN verification. Enter the correct PIN. Click dw(common_apply); . Parent topic: PIN Management

Creating and Sending a Message Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Creating and Sending a Message The dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); allows you to create and send text messages. Procedure Click dw(sms_lable_sms); . Click dw(sms_label_new_message); . Enter a recipient number. NOTE: The dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); enables you to send a message to multiple recipients, whose numbers need to be separated by a semicolon (;). Enter the content of the text message. NOTE: The dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); supports long text messages. When the message you compose exceeds the maximum number of characters allowed for one message, it is split into multiple messages before sending. One long message can be split into up to four messages. Click dw(common_send); . Parent topic: Text Messaging

Creating and Sending a Message Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Creating and Sending a Message The dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); allows you to create and send text messages. Procedure Click dw(sms_lable_sms); . Click dw(sms_label_new_message); . Enter the recipient number in one of the following ways: Click and select the phone number from the phonebook. Enter the phone number of the recipient directly. NOTE: The dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); enables you to send a message to multiple recipients, whose numbers need to be separated by a semicolon (;). Enter the content of the text message. NOTE: The dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); supports long text messages. When the message you compose exceeds the maximum number of characters allowed for one message, it is split into multiple messages before sending. One long message can be split into up to four messages. Click dw(common_send); . Parent topic: Text Messaging

Checking the SkyTone Status Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Checking the SkyTone Status Before you use the SkyTone service, check that the service status is normal. Procedure Choose dw(common_settings); > dw(IDS_tianjitong_text); . Click dw(IDS_vsim_check_status); . Possible query results and the corresponding countermeasures are as follows: Query Result Countermeasure dw(IDS_connection_status); Make sure you have Internet access before you inquire the SkyTone status. dw(IDS_vsim_check_status1); You can use SkyTone. No countermeasure is required. dw(IDS_vsim_check_status0); Do as follows: Restart the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); . If the problem persists, contact Huawei customer service. Parent topic: SkyTone

Using the USSD Function Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Using the USSD Function The Unstructured Supplementary Service Data (USSD) services are interactive data services. You can use an interactive data service supplied by the network operator after sending an USSD command from the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); . Context USSD commands are supplied by the network operator. Contact your network operator for details about their USSD services charging scheme. Procedure Choose dw(ussd_label_ussd); > dw(ussd_label_Universal_title); . Enter the USSD command in the dw(ussd_label_Universal_description); text box or select the corresponding command name. Click dw(common_send); . Parent topic: USSD

Online Update Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Online Update The dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); provides the online update function. Procedure Click dw(common_updates); to check the current version information. NOTE: During your update, do not close the browser or unplug the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); . When detecting a new version and being updated, the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); reports the International Mobile Equipment Identity (IMEI) encrypted by RSA to the online update server. A dw(IDS_update_label_downloading); dialog is displayed, showing the download progress. A dw(common_confirm); dialog is displayed, indicating that the download succeeds. Click dw(common_ok); . Setting VPN Parameters Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Setting VPN Parameters After you set the VPN parameters on the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); , you can use VPN to access your company's intranet from your device, such as a laptop. Procedure Choose dw(common_settings); > dw(IDS_vpn); > dw(IDS_vpn_label_setting); . In the dw(IDS_statistic_label_connection_type); drop-down list, choose dw(vpn_connect_type_L2Tp); . Set the VPN parameters. Parameter Description dw(IDS_VPN_Settings_lns_address); IP address of the Layer 2 Tunneling Protocol (L2TP) network server. L2TP is a virtual tunneling protocol used in virtual private networks. If you are connecting to a VPN server that adopts the L2TP, contact your VPN server provider for settings of dw(IDS_VPN_Settings_lns_address); , dw(IDS_VPN_Settings_tunnelpassword); , dw(IDS_VPN_Settings_pppusername); , and dw(IDS_VPN_Settings_ppppassword); . dw(wlan_label_host_name); Provided by your VPN server provider. dw(IDS_VPN_Settings_tunnelpassword); Provided by your VPN server provider. dw(IDS_VPN_Settings_hellointerval); After a VPN connection is set up, the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); will send status reports to the VPN server at this interval. If the VPN server does not receive the status report for an extended period of time, the VPN connection will terminate. dw(IDS_VPN_Settings_pppusername); Provided by your VPN server provider. dw(IDS_VPN_Settings_ppppassword); Provided by your VPN server provider. dw(wlan_label_authentication); Choose a Point-to-Point Protocol authentication method: CHAP : Challenge Handshake Authentication Protocol (CHAP) is a method of periodically verifying the identity of the peer using a 3-way handshake. During the establishment of a link, the authenticator sends a "challenge" message to the peer. The peer responds with a value calculated using a "one-way hash" function. The authenticator checks the response against its own calculation of the expected hash value. If the values match, the authentication is acknowledged. CHAP provides protection against playback attack. PAP : Password Authentication Protocol (PAP) is a method of using explicit user name and password to verify the identity of a user who attempts to log in to a PPP server. PAP is the authentication methods for most common services, such as bank cards, email accounts, and ADSL devices. It is recommended that you choose PAP. Parent topic: VPN

Configuring an SIP Server Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Configuring an SIP Server Configure parameters for the SIP registration and agent servers according to the instructions on this page. Procedure Choose dw(common_settings); > dw(IDS_VOIP_menu_label_voip); > dw(IDS_VOIP_menu_label_sipserver); . In dw(IDS_VOIP_ProxyServerAdd); , enter the IP address or domain name provided by your service provider. In dw(IDS_VOIP_ProxyServerPor); , enter the port provided by your service provider. In dw(IDS_VOIP_RegServerAddr); , enter the IP address or domain name of the registration server provided by your service provider. Adding an SIP Account Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Adding an SIP Account Add an SIP account according to the instructions on this page. Prerequisite Before you configure an SIP account, configure the SIP server. For details, see Configuring an SIP Server . Procedure Choose dw(common_settings); > dw(IDS_VOIP_menu_label_voip); > dw(IDS_VOIP_menu_label_sipuser); . Click dw(common_add); . In dw(IDS_VOIP_SIPID); , enter the account number provided by your service provider. In dw(dialup_label_user_name); and dw(common_password); , enter your user name and password provided by your service provider for voice services. Click dw(common_ok); . PAGE TEXT:
Enabling or Disabling the SSID Broadcast Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Enabling or Disabling the SSID Broadcast If the service set identifier (SSID) broadcast function is enabled, the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); broadcasts the SSID of the wireless local area network (WLAN) facilitating clients' access to the WLAN. The disadvantage is that unauthorized clients can also access the WLAN. If the SSID broadcast is disabled, the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); does not broadcast the SSID of the WLAN. So the SSID must be entered manually and correctly from a client before the client accesses the WLAN. This improves the security of the WLAN. Procedure Choose dw(common_settings); > dw(wlan_label_wlan); > dw(wlan_label_wlan_basic_settings); . From the SSID list, choose the SSID you want to change, and click dw(common_edit); . Set dw(wlan_label_ssid_broadcast); . dw(common_enable); : to enable the SSID broadcast. dw(common_disable); : to disable the SSID broadcast. Click dw(common_apply); . NOTE: During the process of setting up the WLAN, you can set the SSID broadcast to dw(common_enable); to facilitate the access from clients. After the WLAN is set up, set the SSID broadcast to dw(common_disable); to improve the security of the WLAN. Parent topic: Setting the SSID of the WLAN

Setting the WLAN Key Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Setting the WLAN Key To improve the security of the wireless local area network (WLAN), set a security key for the WLAN. Procedure Choose dw(common_settings); > dw(wlan_label_wlan); > dw(wlan_label_wlan_basic_settings); . From the SSID list, choose the SSID you want to change, and click dw(common_edit); . Select dw(IDS_wlan_label_80211_authentication); . dw(IDS_wlan_label_open); : No encryption. No security key is required when clients access the WLAN. NOTE: For daily use, it is recommended that you set a security key, to improve the security performance of a WLAN. dw(wlan_label_wep); : This stands for Wired Equivalent Privacy. dw(wlan_label_wpa2_psk); : This is the second version of dw(wlan_label_wpa_psk); and is more secure than dw(wlan_label_wpa_psk); . dw(wlan_label_wpa_wpa2_psk); : Both dw(wlan_label_wpa_psk); and dw(wlan_label_wpa2_psk); encryption modes are supported. Clients can access the WLAN in dw(wlan_label_wpa_psk); or dw(wlan_label_wpa2_psk); mode. Enter a security key. NOTE: Select dw(common_show_password); to view the security key that you have entered. Click dw(common_apply); . Setting a WPA Security Key for the WLAN From dw(IDS_wlan_label_80211_authentication); , select dw(wlan_label_wpa2_psk); . In dw(wlan_label_wpa_preshared_key); , enter the security key. For example, 12345678. Click dw(common_apply); . Parent topic: WLAN Settings

Disabling the WLAN Automatically Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Disabling the WLAN Automatically If the function of automatically disabling the wireless local area network (WLAN) is enabled, the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); disables the WLAN when all the following conditions are met: The dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); is powered by only the battery; the WLAN is enabled; no clients access the WLAN for a period of time; the idle time exceeds the value of dw(dialup_label_max_idle_time_s); . To use the WLAN, re-enable the WLAN. Procedure Choose dw(common_settings); > dw(wlan_label_wlan); > dw(wlan_label_wlan_advanced_settings); . From dw(wlan_label_wlan_auto_off); , select: dw(common_enable); : to enable the function of automatically disabling the WLAN. dw(common_disable); : to disable the function of automatically disabling the WLAN. From dw(dialup_label_max_idle_time_s); , select a time period. Click dw(common_apply); . Parent topic: WLAN Settings

Setting AP Isolation Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Setting AP Isolation If access point (AP) isolation is enabled, clients connected to the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); cannot communicate with each other. Setting WLAN MAC Filtering Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Setting WLAN MAC Filtering You can control and manage the clients that access the wireless local area network (WLAN) to improve the security performance of the WLAN. Procedure Choose dw(common_settings); > dw(wlan_label_wlan); > dw(wlan_label_wlan_mac_filter); . From dw(wlan_label_wlan_mac_filter); , select a Media Access Control (MAC) filtering mode. dw(common_disable); : to disable the function of filtering MAC addresses. dw(wlan_label_allow); : If the MAC address of a client is listed in dw(wlan_label_mac_address); , the client is allowed to connect to the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); over the WLAN. dw(wlan_label_deny); : If the MAC address of a client is listed in dw(wlan_label_mac_address); , the client is prohibited from connecting to the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); over the WLAN. In dw(wlan_label_mac_address); , enter the MAC addresses of the clients to be controlled. Click dw(common_apply); . Allowing Specified Clients to Access the WLAN Click dw(connection_hilink_label_traffic_statistics); . From the dw(wlan_label_mac_address); column under dw(wlan_label_wifi_clients); , find the MAC addresses of clients whose access to the WLAN is allowed. For example, 40:4D:8E:6D:80:7D. Choose dw(common_settings); > dw(wlan_label_wlan); > dw(wlan_label_wlan_mac_filter); . From dw(wlan_label_wlan_mac_filter); , select dw(wlan_label_allow); . In dw(wlan_label_mac_address); , enter 40:4D:8E:6D:80:7D . Click dw(common_apply); . Parent topic: WLAN Settings

Specifying the WLAN Bandwidth Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Specifying the WLAN Bandwidth The wireless local area network (WLAN) bandwidth is the transmission frequency bandwidth of the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); . A greater transmission frequency bandwidth indicates a faster data transmission speed and lower penetrability. Procedure Choose dw(common_settings); > dw(wlan_label_wlan); > dw(wlan_label_wlan_advanced_settings); . Select the desired WLAN bandwidth from the dw(wlan_label_wlan_band_width); drop-down list box. dw(common_auto); : The dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); selects the most appropriate bandwidth based on the actual network situation. 20M : This option indicates 20 MHz, corresponds to the bandwidth of 65 Mbit/s, and produces good penetrability and long transmission distance. Click dw(common_apply); . Parent topic: WLAN Settings

Connecting a Client to the <script>dw(common_3g_wireless_modem);</script> Through PIN Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Connecting a Client to the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); Through PIN In Personal Information Number (PIN) mode, you can securely connect a client to the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); 's WLAN by simply entering the client's PIN on the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); 's web management page. You can then access the Internet through the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); . Procedure Set the client's WPS connection method to PIN, and record the PIN displayed on the client. NOTE: By default, dw(IDS_wps_function_isenable); is set to dw(common_on); . If dw(IDS_wps_function_isenable); is set to dw(common_off); , WPS connection mode cannot be set. Choose dw(IDS_wps_input_pin); . Enter the client's PIN in the dw(IDS_common_pin); text box. Click dw(dialup_label_mobile_connection); . Parent topic: WPS

PAGE TEXT:
WLAN Settings Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations WLAN Settings Enabling or Disabling WLAN This section describes how to enable or disable dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); 's WLAN. Setting the SSID of the WLAN The service set identifier (SSID) is a name that identifies a wireless local area network (WLAN). A wireless client (for example, a computer) can communicate with the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); properly only when they are using the same SSID. To ensure the WLAN security, do not use the default SSID. You can define an SSID as required. Setting the WLAN Key To improve the security of the wireless local area network (WLAN), set a security key for the WLAN. Disabling the WLAN Automatically If the function of automatically disabling the wireless local area network (WLAN) is enabled, the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); disables the WLAN when all the following conditions are met: The dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); is powered by only the battery; the WLAN is enabled; no clients access the WLAN for a period of time; the idle time exceeds the value of dw(dialup_label_max_idle_time_s); . To use the WLAN, re-enable the WLAN. Selecting a Country and a Channel This section describes how to select a required country and channel. Selecting a Channel This section describes how to select a channel. Setting the Working Band This section describes how to select a required work band. Setting AP Isolation If access point (AP) isolation is enabled, clients connected to the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); cannot communicate with each other. If AP isolation is disabled, clients connected to the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); can communicate with each other. WPS The Wi-Fi Protected Setup (WPS) is a standard used to set up wireless connections in an easy and secure way. Traditionally speaking, to set up a wireless connection, you have to set a WLAN name (SSID) and key for the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); , and enter the key on the client. WPS automatically configures an SSID and key for the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); and client. You can conveniently and securely connect your client to the WLAN without having to remember the SSID and key. Setting WLAN MAC Filtering You can control and manage the clients that access the wireless local area network (WLAN) to improve the security performance of the WLAN. Specifying the WLAN Bandwidth The wireless local area network (WLAN) bandwidth is the transmission frequency bandwidth of the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); . A greater transmission frequency bandwidth indicates a faster data transmission speed and lower penetrability. Parent topic: User Manual

Setting the SSID of the WLAN Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Setting the SSID of the WLAN The service set identifier (SSID) is a name that identifies a wireless local area network (WLAN). A wireless client (for example, a computer) can communicate with the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); properly only when they are using the same SSID. To ensure the WLAN security, do not use the default SSID. You can define an SSID as required. Changing the SSID This section describes how to change the service set identifier (SSID). Enabling or Disabling the SSID Broadcast If the service set identifier (SSID) broadcast function is enabled, the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); broadcasts the SSID of the wireless local area network (WLAN) facilitating clients' access to the WLAN. The disadvantage is that unauthorized clients can also access the WLAN. If the SSID broadcast is disabled, the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); does not broadcast the SSID of the WLAN. So the SSID must be entered manually and correctly from a client before the client accesses the WLAN. This improves the security of the WLAN. Parent topic: WLAN Settings

WPS Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations WPS The Wi-Fi Protected Setup (WPS) is a standard used to set up wireless connections in an easy and secure way. Traditionally speaking, to set up a wireless connection, you have to set a WLAN name (SSID) and key for the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); , and enter the key on the client. WPS automatically configures an SSID and key for the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); and client. You can conveniently and securely connect your client to the WLAN without having to remember the SSID and key. Connecting a Client to the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); Through PIN In Personal Information Number (PIN) mode, you can securely connect a client to the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); 's WLAN by simply entering the client's PIN on the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); 's web management page. You can then access the Internet through the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); . Connecting a Client to the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); Through PBC In Push Button Configuration (PBC) mode, you can connect a client to the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); 's WLAN simply by pushing the WPS button. You can then access the Internet through the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); . Connecting a Client to the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); Through AP PIN In AP Personal Information Number (PIN) mode, you can securely connect a client to the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); 's WLAN by simply entering the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); 's PIN on the client. You can then access the Internet through the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); . Parent topic: WLAN Settings

Connection Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Connection To use the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); to access the Internet, you must create a network profile or set the default network profile and operator's network. Creating a Network Profile If you are using the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); for the first time, you must create a network profile to access the Internet. Setting the Default Network Profile After you set the default profile, the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); accesses the Internet via the default operator's network. Setting the Operator's Network After configuring the settings, the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); can quickly find networks. Accessing the Internet Using the Cellular Data Network Use the following method to connect the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); to the Internet. Accessing the Internet Using a WLAN The dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); can connect to the Internet using a wireless local area network (WLAN) to reduce cellular network data usage. Connecting to Other Devices Using Bluetooth Bluetooth is a wireless technology for short-distance (generally 10 meters) communication between devices. The dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); can exchange data with clients using Bluetooth. Connecting Clients to the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); over a WLAN After WLAN is enabled on the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); , clients can connect to the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); and access the Internet using it. Viewing the Clients Connected Check the number of clients connected to the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); and their details. Adding a Client to the Blacklist On the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); 's web management page, you can add unwanted clients connected to the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); to the blacklist to better secure the Wi-Fi network. Parent topic: User Manual

Accessing the Internet Using a WLAN Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Accessing the Internet Using a WLAN The dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); can connect to the Internet using a wireless local area network (WLAN) to reduce cellular network data usage. Connecting to a Free WLAN You may find free wireless local area networks (WLANs) in public places, such as airports, hotels, and libraries. Connecting to a Secure WLAN To connect to a secure wireless local area network (WLAN), you need to enter the service set identifier (SSID) and password of that network. Setting a WLAN's Priority The dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); will automatically connects to a WLAN based on the priorities you set. Setting the WLAN Password This section describes how to set the password used to access a wireless local area network (WLAN). Parent topic: Connection

PAGE TEXT:
Clients Cannot Access the Internet Properly Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Clients Cannot Access the Internet Properly Procedure Check that clients are connected to the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); properly. Check that the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); is powered on properly. Check that the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); is in a location covered by communication networks and that the signal strength is strong. Check that the network mode meets the requirement of the local service provider. For details, see Setting the Operator's Network . When the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); is used for Internet access, the Point-to-Point Protocol (PPP) user name and password must be set correctly. Check that the PPP user name and password are correct. For details, see Creating a Network Profile . If the Dynamic Host Configuration Protocol (DHCP) service is not enabled and the clients are set to obtain IP addresses dynamically, the clients cannot access the Internet. In this case, enable the DHCP service. For details, see DHCP Settings . Check that the network adapters of the clients are working properly. Contact your service provider if the problem persists. Parent topic: FAQs

PAGE TEXT:
Clients Cannot Access the WLAN Properly Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Clients Cannot Access the WLAN Properly Procedure Check whether there are potential interference sources or shielding objects near the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); . If any, adjust the placement of the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); . Check and record the settings of the following parameters on the clients and the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); : dw(wlan_label_ssid); , dw(wlan_label_encryption_mode); , and security key. The service set identifier (SSID) of the clients must be set to ANY or be same as that set on the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); . The encryption mode and the security key on the clients must match those set on the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); . Otherwise, change the settings on the clients. If the Media Access Control (MAC) address filtering in the wireless local area network (WLAN) is enabled on the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); , check that the MAC addresses of the clients are not in the dw(wlan_label_deny); list. For details, see Setting WLAN MAC Filtering . Parent topic: FAQs

Multi-Network IP Address Conflict Occurs Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Multi-Network IP Address Conflict Occurs When multiple types of Internet connections are used simultaneously on the client, such as physical network adapter and Wi-Fi, inserting the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); into the client may result in IP address conflict across multiple networks. PAGE TEXT:
All Website Addresses Entered in the Browser Are Redirected to the <script>dw(common_3g_wireless_modem);</script> 's Homepage Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations All Website Addresses Entered in the Browser Are Redirected to the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); 's Homepage When the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); is inserted to a client where other network adapters exist, all websites entered in the browser will be redirected to the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); 's homepage. Procedure Check whether the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); is accessing the Internet using dial-up connection. If yes, you do not need to address the problem, because in this case all website addresses will be redirected to the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); 's homepage. If not, remove the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); , or set up a dial-up connection. Parent topic: FAQs

What Can I Do If the Network Connection Is Frequently Interrupted? Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations What Can I Do If the Network Connection Is Frequently Interrupted? Procedure Check the network signal strength. If the signal is weak, move the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); somewhere with better reception. Visit other websites to check whether the issue lies within a particular site. Close all other applications that require network connections. Contact your Internet service provider if the problem persists. Parent topic: FAQs

Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations What Can I Do If I Cannot Open Web Pages After a Network Connection Is Set Up? Procedure Re-set up a dial-up Internet connection. Check that the computer is not infected with viruses. Check that the APN user name and password in the profile management settings are correct. Why can't I charge the <script>dw(common_3g_wireless_modem);</script> through USB? Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Why can't I charge the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); through USB? Symptom: When you charge the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); through USB, the charging icon is displayed, but the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); 's power level does not increase. When you charge the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); through USB, if it is downloading files at high speeds or transmitting a large amount of data, the power consumption will exceed the USB power supply. In this case, it is recommended that you use the standard charger to charge your dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); . Parent topic: FAQs

Enabling or Disabling the Firewall Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Enabling or Disabling the Firewall The dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); supports the firewall function to control the transmission of data streams and protect your local area network from unauthorized access. Procedure Choose dw(common_settings); > dw(firewall_label_security); > dw(firewall_label_firewall_switch); . Select dw(firewall_label_firewall_enable); to enable the firewall or clear it to disable the firewall. NOTE: Other parameters are available only if you select dw(firewall_label_firewall_enable); . Select dw(firewall_label_ip_address_filter_enable); to enable the function of filtering IP addresses. Select dw(firewall_label_wan_port_ping_disable); to disable the ping function. Select dw(IDS_firewall_macfilter_enable); to enable the MAC filtering function. Click dw(common_apply); . Parent topic: Security Settings

Setting LAN IP Filtering Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Setting LAN IP Filtering The function to filter IP addresses in the local area network (LAN) can be used to restrict the access to specific Internet services from specified clients in the LAN. Prerequisite The IP address filtering function is enabled. For details, see Enabling or Disabling the Firewall . Context The following table lists common protocols at the Application Layer in the Transmission Control Protocol and the Internet Protocol (TCP/IP). Protocol Default Port Service Provided HTTP 80 Browse Web pages. SMTP 25 Send emails. POP3 110 Receive emails. FTP 21 Transmit files. Telnet 23 Log in to remote computers. NOTE: For details about the ports used for specific services, contact the relevant service providers. Procedure Choose dw(common_settings); > dw(firewall_label_security); > dw(firewall_label_lan_ip_filter); . Click dw(common_add); and set the parameters. Click dw(common_ok); to save the settings. NOTE: dw(common_edit); : to edit an item. dw(common_delete); : to delete an item. Click dw(common_apply); for the settings to take effect. Blocking a Client from Accessing a Specified Website To block the client whose IP address is 192.168.1.101 in the wireless local area network (WLAN) from accessing www.abc.com, do the following: Choose dw(common_settings); > dw(firewall_label_security); > dw(firewall_label_lan_ip_filter); . Click dw(common_add); and set the parameters. dw(firewall_label_lan_ip_address); Enter 192.168.1.101 . NOTE: You can view the IP addresses of clients connected to the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); on the dw(connection_hilink_label_traffic_statistics); page under dw(wlan_label_wifi_clients); . dw(firewall_label_lan_port); Enter 80 . dw(firewall_label_wan_ip_address); From the operating system, choose Start > Run . Enter cmd and press Enter . In the displayed command-line interface, enter ping www.abc.com and press Enter . View the IP address of www.abc.com . For example, it is 1.2.3.4 . In the text box for dw(firewall_label_wan_ip_address); on the relevant page of the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); , enter 1.2.3.4 . NOTE: The Windows 7 operating system is used in this example to describe how to obtain wide area network (WAN) IP addresses. dw(firewall_label_wan_port); Enter 80 . dw(firewall_label_protocol); Select dw(firewall_label_tcp_or_udp); . NOTE: If you do not know the protocol, select dw(firewall_label_tcp_or_udp); . The dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); will automatically select an appropriate protocol. dw(firewall_label_status); Select dw(common_on); . dw(common_options); Click dw(common_ok); . Click dw(common_apply); for the settings to take effect. Parent topic: Security Settings

Configuring a Virtual Server Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Configuring a Virtual Server The dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); supports the virtual server to enable external users to use the services provided in the local area network (LAN) using the Hypertext Transfer Protocol (HTTP), File Transfer Protocol (FTP), and other protocols. Procedure Choose dw(common_settings); > dw(firewall_label_security); > dw(firewall_label_virtual_server); . Click dw(common_add); and set the parameters. Click dw(common_ok); to save the settings. NOTE: dw(common_edit); : to edit an item. dw(common_delete); : to delete an item. Click dw(common_apply); for the settings to take effect. Configuring an FTP Server To enable a client whose IP address is 192.168.8.101 to provide FTP services and be accessed by external users, do the following: Configure a virtual server. dw(firewall_label_name); dw(firewall_label_wan_port); dw(firewall_label_lan_ip_address); dw(firewall_label_lan_port); dw(firewall_label_protocol); dw(firewall_label_status); My FTP server 21 192.168.8.101 21 dw(firewall_label_tcp); dw(common_on); Choose dw(common_settings); > dw(system_label_system); > dw(system_label_device_information); , view the wide area network (WAN) IP address of the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); . For example 10.2.1.123. In the browser of an external user, enter the FTP server address ( ftp://10.2.1.123 ) and access the FTP services provided by the client whose IP address is 192.168.8.101. NOTE: By default, the FTP service port is 21. If this port is changed (for example, it is changed to 8021), the external user must enter ftp://10.2.1.123:8021 to access the FTP server. Configuring a Special Application Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Configuring a Special Application The dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); supports the function to use a special application to configure dynamic port forwarding. Certain applications in the local area network (LAN) must use a specified port of the firewall to access remote applications. To set up a Transmission Control Protocol and User Datagram Protocol (TCP/UDP) connection between an application in the LAN and a remote application, the firewall uses this port forwarding function to open the required port. Procedure Choose dw(common_settings); > dw(firewall_label_security); > dw(firewall_label_special_application); . Click dw(common_add); and set the parameters. Click dw(common_ok); to save the settings. NOTE: dw(common_edit); : to edit an item. dw(common_delete); : to delete an item. Click dw(common_apply); for the settings to take effect. Example of the Configuration of a Special Application A client in the LAN uses TCP to access the MSN game server through port 47624. When the game starts, the game server uses TCP and port 2400 to set up a connection to the client that tries to access the server. In this case, you must configure dynamic port forwarding, because the game conflicts with the following default firewall rules: The firewall blocks external data input. The game server can send a connection request to the external IP address of the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); but cannot send the request to the LAN client that tries to access the game server, because the IP addresses of clients are not open to external devices. To solve this problem, you must define a set of port forwarding rules. When the client in the LAN sends data to TCP port 47624, the rules allow data input from TCP port 2400. Then data from the game server can be received from and transmitted to the LAN client that sends data to TCP port 47624. dw(firewall_label_name); dw(firewall_label_status); dw(firewall_label_trigger_port); dw(firewall_label_trigger_protocol); dw(firewall_label_open_protocol); dw(firewall_label_open_port); MSN Gaming Zone dw(common_on); 47624 dw(firewall_label_tcp); dw(firewall_label_tcp); 2400 NOTE: For details about dw(firewall_label_trigger_port); , dw(firewall_label_open_port); , dw(firewall_label_trigger_protocol); , and dw(firewall_label_open_protocol); , contact your service provider. Parent topic: Security Settings

Setting the DMZ Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Setting the DMZ If external users cannot access certain network services provided by the local area network (LAN), use the DMZ function provided by the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); to set the client that provides the required network services as the DMZ host; external users can then access these services properly. DMZ is an acronym for the demilitarized zone in networks. Prerequisite DMZ users are not protected by the firewall and may be easily attacked. In addition, the security of other users in the LAN is compromised. Procedure Choose dw(common_settings); > dw(firewall_label_security); > dw(firewall_label_dmz_settings); . Select: dw(common_enable); : to enable the DMZ function. dw(common_disable); : to disable the DMZ function. In dw(firewall_label_dmz_ip_address); , enter the IP address of the client to be set as the DMZ host. NOTE: One client can be set as the DMZ host at a time. Click dw(common_apply); . Parent topic: Security Settings

Setting the SIP ALG Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Setting the SIP ALG The Session Initiation Protocol (SIP) is a control protocol at the Application Layer. It is used to initiate, change, or end a session. An application-level gateway (ALG) is a specific application of SIP and is used to check the status of data packages. To complete a SIP application, enable the SIP ALG. Procedure Choose dw(common_settings); > dw(firewall_label_security); > dw(firewall_label_sip_alg_settings); . Select dw(firewall_label_enable_sip_alg); to enable the SIP ALG. In dw(firewall_label_sip_port); , enter the SIP port number provided by your service provider. Click dw(common_apply); . PAGE TEXT:
Configuring NAT Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Configuring NAT Network address translation is the process of modifying source and destination IP addresses when IP packets are transmitted across a router or firewall. The purpose of this process is to translate internal (private) IP addresses into external (public) IP addresses to provide a solution to the impending exhaustion of IP addresses. dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); supports port-restricted cone NAT and symmetric NAT. You can configure the NAT settings as required. Procedure Choose dw(common_settings); > dw(firewall_label_security); > dw(IDS_wlan_title_nat_settings); . Select from the following options: dw(IDS_wlan_option_nat_cone); : to enable port-restricted cone NAT. This type of NAT are more compatible with applications (including applications on game devices), although it provides lower security. dw(IDS_wlan_option_nat_symmetric); : to enable symmetric NAT. This type of NAT are generally adopted by gateways with higher security. Click dw(common_apply); . Parent topic: Security Settings

Filtering Specified Devices Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Filtering Specified Devices The MAC filtering function of the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); can filter specified devices on the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); local area network (LAN) so these devices are prevented from accessing the Internet or other devices on the same LAN. Procedure Choose dw(common_settings); > dw(firewall_label_security); > dw(IDS_security_macfilter_label); . Select an MAC filtering mode from the dw(IDS_security_macfilter_lbl_filter_mode); drop-down list box. dw(common_disable); : Disable the MAC filtering function. dw(wlan_label_allow); : If the MAC address of a client is in the dw(wlan_label_mac_address); list, the client is allowed to connect to the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); . dw(wlan_label_deny); : If the MAC address of a client is in the dw(wlan_label_mac_address); list, the client is not allowed to connect to the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); . In the dw(wlan_label_mac_address); text box, enter the MAC address of the client you want to filter. Click dw(common_apply); . Parent topic: Security Settings

PAGE TEXT:
Security Settings Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Security Settings Enabling or Disabling the Firewall The dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); supports the firewall function to control the transmission of data streams and protect your local area network from unauthorized access. Setting LAN IP Filtering The function to filter IP addresses in the local area network (LAN) can be used to restrict the access to specific Internet services from specified clients in the LAN. Configuring a Virtual Server The dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); supports the virtual server to enable external users to use the services provided in the local area network (LAN) using the Hypertext Transfer Protocol (HTTP), File Transfer Protocol (FTP), and other protocols. Configuring a Special Application The dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); supports the function to use a special application to configure dynamic port forwarding. Certain applications in the local area network (LAN) must use a specified port of the firewall to access remote applications. To set up a Transmission Control Protocol and User Datagram Protocol (TCP/UDP) connection between an application in the LAN and a remote application, the firewall uses this port forwarding function to open the required port. Setting the DMZ If external users cannot access certain network services provided by the local area network (LAN), use the DMZ function provided by the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); to set the client that provides the required network services as the DMZ host; external users can then access these services properly. DMZ is an acronym for the demilitarized zone in networks. Setting the SIP ALG The Session Initiation Protocol (SIP) is a control protocol at the Application Layer. It is used to initiate, change, or end a session. An application-level gateway (ALG) is a specific application of SIP and is used to check the status of data packages. To complete a SIP application, enable the SIP ALG. Setting the UPnP The Universal Plug and Play (UPnP) service realizes intelligent connection between two UPnP devices using port forwarding. UPnP devices can obtain IP addresses automatically and access the Internet dynamically. Configuring NAT Network address translation is the process of modifying source and destination IP addresses when IP packets are transmitted across a router or firewall. The purpose of this process is to translate internal (private) IP addresses into external (public) IP addresses to provide a solution to the impending exhaustion of IP addresses. dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); supports port-restricted cone NAT and symmetric NAT. You can configure the NAT settings as required. Filtering Specified Devices The MAC filtering function of the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); can filter specified devices on the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); local area network (LAN) so these devices are prevented from accessing the Internet or other devices on the same LAN. Parent topic: User Manual

Setting the Web Sharing Mode Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Setting the Web Sharing Mode You can set dw(sd_label_access_type); and dw(sd_shared_path); under dw(sd_label_web_share_mode); . Procedure Click dw(sharing_label_sharing); . Under dw(sd_label_sd_card_sharing); , select dw(sd_label_web_share_mode); . Under dw(sd_websharemode_settings); , set the web sharing mode. Set dw(sd_label_access_type); . dw(sd_label_read_only); : Shared files can be viewed only. dw(sd_label_read_write); : Shared files can be viewed and managed. Set dw(sd_shared_path); . dw(sd_label_entire_sd_card); : indicates that all files stored in the microSD card can be accessed. dw(sd_label_custom_setting); : indicates that certain specified files in the microSD card can be accessed. Click dw(firewall_label_select); , and select folders in the dw(sd_hint_select_a_folder); dialog box to share the files in those folders. Click dw(common_apply); . Parent topic: Accessing the microSD Card over the Network

Uploading Files to Be Shared Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Uploading Files to Be Shared When the dw(sd_label_web_share_mode); access type is set to dw(sd_label_read_write); , you can create a folder in the microSD card, and upload local files to it. Procedure Click dw(sharing_label_sharing); . Under dw(sd_label_sd_card_sharing); , select dw(sd_label_web_share_mode); . In the dw(sd_label_upload_file); dialog box, click dw(common_browse); , and select the local files to be uploaded. Click dw(sd_label_upload); . Accessing the microSD Card over the Network Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Accessing the microSD Card over the Network When dw(sd_label_web_share_mode); is enabled, you can access the microSD card from the web management page. Setting the Web Sharing Mode You can set dw(sd_label_access_type); and dw(sd_shared_path); under dw(sd_label_web_share_mode); . Uploading Files to Be Shared When the dw(sd_label_web_share_mode); access type is set to dw(sd_label_read_write); , you can create a folder in the microSD card, and upload local files to it. Viewing and Downloading Shared Files You can view and download shared files stored on the microSD card. Deleting Shared Files When the dw(sd_label_web_share_mode); access type is set to dw(sd_label_read_write); , you can delete files stored on the microSD card. Parent topic: Sharing the microSD Card

Setting the Monthly Traffic Statistics Function Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Setting the Monthly Traffic Statistics Function You can set the monthly traffic statistics function and view the network traffic of the current month. Procedure Click dw(connection_hilink_label_traffic_statistics); . Click dw(IDS_statistics_aglie_set_package); . The dw(IDS_statistics_aglie_set_package_dialog); dialog box is displayed. Set the monthly traffic statistics parameters. NOTE: If your traffic usage in the current month exceeds dw(IDS_statistics_aglie_data_threshold); of dw(IDS_statistics_aglie_set_data_volume_mobile); , the bar color on the dw(connection_hilink_label_traffic_statistics); page changes. Pay attention to your traffic usage to avoid incurring high data costs. Click dw(common_save); . NOTE: Click dw(IDS_statistics_aglie_edit_month_volue); to modify the monthly traffic statistics parameters. Parent topic: Traffic Statistics

Changing the Password Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Changing the Password To prevent unauthorized access to the management page, change the login password. Procedure Choose dw(common_settings); > dw(system_label_system); > dw(system_label_modify_password); . Enter the current password. Enter a new password and confirm it. NOTE: The password can contain a maximum of 15 characters, including numbers, letters (upper or lower case), space, and the following symbols: ! # $ ( ) * - . / = @ [ ] ^ _ ` { } ~ | Click dw(common_apply); . PAGE TEXT:
Performing a Ping Test Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Performing a Ping Test If the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); cannot connect to the Internet, perform a ping test to identify the possible causes. Procedure Choose dw(common_settings); > dw(system_label_system); > dw(system_label_diagnosis); . Select dw(IDS_system_label_diagnosis_method_ping); from the dw(IDS_system_diagnosis_method_name); drop-down list box. In the dw(IDS_system_label_diagnosis_targetip_domain); text box, enter the IP address or domain name, for example www.google.com. Set dw(IDS_system_label_diagnosis_packetsize); and dw(IDS_system_label_diagnosis_timeout); . Select or deselect dw(IDS_system_label_diagnosis_fragment_remind); . NOTE: Select dw(IDS_system_label_diagnosis_fragment_remind); if you set dw(IDS_system_label_diagnosis_packetsize); to a value greater than its default value. Click dw(common_apply); . The diagnostics results are displayed in the dw(IDS_diagnosis_result_title); area on the bottom of the page. Parent topic: Diagnosing Network Connection Exceptions

Performing a Traceroute Test Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Performing a Traceroute Test If the dw(common_3g_wireless_modem); cannot connect to the Internet, perform a traceroute test to identify the possible causes. Procedure Choose dw(common_settings); > dw(system_label_system); > dw(system_label_diagnosis); . Select dw(IDS_system_label_diagnosis_method_traceroute); from the dw(IDS_system_diagnosis_method_name); drop-down list box. In the dw(IDS_system_label_diagnosis_targetip_domain); text box, enter the IP address or domain name, for example www.google.com. Set dw(IDS_system_label_diagnosis_maximum_hops); and dw(IDS_system_label_diagnosis_timeout); . Click dw(common_apply); . The diagnostics results are displayed in the dw(IDS_diagnosis_result_title); area on the bottom of the page. Parent topic: Diagnosing Network Connection Exceptions

Setting User Rights Copyright Statement Logging In to the Management Page Status Icons Quick Setup SkyTone Connection VPN Traffic Statistics Text Messaging SMS USSD Update Sharing the microSD Card PIN Management WLAN Settings DHCP Settings Security Settings System Management FAQs Acronyms and Abbreviations Setting User Rights Users will be able to access a USB storage device only after you set the read/write and folder access rights for them. Procedure Choose dw(sharing_label_sharing); > dw(IDS_settingz_samba_user_settings); . Click dw(common_add); . Enter the user name and password. Enter the password again. Set dw(IDS_system_samba_user_setting_right); . dw(sd_label_read_only); : shared files can only be viewed. dw(sd_label_read_write); : shared files can be viewed and modified. Set dw(IDS_system_samba_user_setting_directory); . dw(IDS_system_samba_share_allpath); : all files stored in the USB storage device can be accessed. dw(IDS_system_samba_share_user_path); : certain specified files in the USB storage device can be accessed. Click dw(firewall_label_select); and select folders in the displayed dw(sd_hint_select_a_folder); dialog box. Click dw(common_ok); . Click dw(common_apply); . Parent topic: User Management
